# v7 timeline: Screen_Recording_2026-10-31_at_16.29.19
60 fps analysis grid; 4766 frames; 1622x918 (the picture of a 1622x936 screen recording, top 18 rows of capture bar cropped); classes: GRAPHICS 26, MIXED 8, LIVE 3.
Material: a screen recording of a video player, variable frame rate on the 60 Hz grid: mostly 24 fps material on a 3:2 cadence (new pictures 2-3 frames apart) with 60 Hz duplicate-capture runs and some 30 fps stretches (motion.csv local_fps); durations carry +-1-2 frames of capture jitter. The player's speaker icon at the bottom right is ignored; footage is never described.
Merged from chunks v7c1 (f0-f709), v7c2 (f710-f1629), v7c3 (f1630-f2333), v7c4 (f2334-f3056), v7c5 (f3057-f3870) and v7c6 (f3871-f4765). Every chunk edge falls inside a segment, checked at the merge on the overview sheets: f709/f710 inside one footage shot (S07; sheets/overview_00672-00717.jpg), f1629/f1630 inside the file-browser drag (S13; sheets/overview_01584-01629.jpg, sheets/overview_01632-01677.jpg), f2333/f2334 inside the editor's exit drift (S17; sheets/overview_02304-02349.jpg), f3056/f3057 inside the agent-log pull-back (S26; sheets/overview_03024-03069.jpg) and f3870/f3871 inside the file-tile hold (S31; sheets/overview_03840-03885.jpg); these five segments are stitched from both chunks. Classes: S31 was GRAPHICS for v7c5 and MIXED for v7c6; its thumbnail shows one still picture for the whole segment (re-checked at the merge: f3735 vs f3780 phase-correlation response 0.976 at zero shift, mean abs differences 0.05-0.9 luma between captured pictures against a static background; v7c6 measured 0.19-0.52 luma over f3871-f3887), so it is GRAPHICS like the other still pictures inside UI (S09, S11, S14, S16, S30). S17, S22, S23 and S29 are MIXED because footage plays inside their masked windows and cards (re-checked for S22 and S29: 1.7-4.4 luma mean abs difference between captured pictures inside the card, S29 during its hold f3388-f3423, S22 at f2676-f2696). S32 carries no designed element over f3889-f4070, but no boundary separates that stretch from the badge and captions that follow, so it is one MIXED segment.
Loop: f4522 (c083) is the player's loop point. The recording then shows the video's first 13 frames (f4522-f4534: blank white, then the title label's seed dash) and from f4535 every picture equals f0 onward (+4535 f): S36 replays S01 and S37 replays S02 f157-f230. The recording itself started 13 frames into the video, so S01 starts mid-pop at f0.
Boundaries (36): T01 iris to white f144-f168 (soft, anchor c004) · T02 whip up f269-f304, hidden cut f289 · T03 hard cut f419 · T04 hard cut f484 · T05 hard cut f631 · T06 hard cut f706 · T07 hard cut f900 · T08 hard cut f1079 · T09 whip left f1151-f1226, cut f1196 · T10 spin-zoom whip f1234-f1281, cut f1259 · T11 whip left f1426-f1514, cut f1487 · T12 whip left f1519-f1566, cut f1542 · T13 whip left f1606-f1690, cut f1644 · T14 hard cut f1786 · T15 whip right f2007-f2041 (incoming half, cut f2007) · T16 punch-in whip up f2041-f2120, cut f2091 · T17 whip left f2342-f2371 (incoming half, cut f2342) · T18 whip left f2361-f2424, hidden cut f2392 · T19 punch-in whip up f2444-f2496, hidden cut f2474 · T20 whip left f2499-f2589, hidden cut f2515 · T21 punch-in whip up f2589-f2641, hidden cut f2609 · T22 whip left f2713-f2756, cut f2736 · T23 hard cut f2824 · T24 whip left f2941-f2971 (incoming half, cut f2941) · T25 whip left f2961-f3014, hidden cut f2991 · T26 punch-in whip up f3063-f3114, hidden cut f3094 · T27 whip left f3119-f3184, hidden cut f3134 · T28 whip left f3191-f3246, hidden cut f3214 · T29 down-left whip into a zoom-in f3535-f3624, cut f3581 · T30 zoom-through f3651-f3716, cut f3690 · T31 hard cut f3889 · T32 hard cut f4216 · T33 punch-in whip zoom f4277-f4307, hidden cut f4289 · T34 hard cut f4369 · T35 hard cut f4522 (player loop point) · T36 iris to white f4679-f4703 (soft, anchor c090).
34 of the 36 boundaries are hard cuts (the picture is replaced between two consecutive pictures); 22 of them sit inside a whip or zoom whose full motion range is recorded with curves (segments.json type whip or zoom-through, with cut_frame), and those segments are tiled at the cut frame, as every chunk did; the two iris wipes are tiled at their midpoints (S01 ends f156, S36 ends f4691). Nine hard cuts were missed by all three detectors (f289, f2392, f2474, f2515, f2991, f3094, f3134, f3214, f4289: the same canvas jumping in scale, or similar frames) and are the manual entries m001-m009 in cuts.json; of the 90 detector candidates 27 are confirmed and 63 rejected (camera moves inside footage, in-window scrolls, fx inside a shot, and PySceneDetect index echoes of real cuts, which on this VFR file land 1-10 captured pictures early or late).
Rhythm: 114.84 BPM (31.35 f per beat, audio.json). 12 of the 36 boundary frames lie within 3 f of a beat (f289 +2, f484 +1, f706 0, f1487 -3, f1644 +2, f1786 -3, f2007 +2, f2091 -2, f2392 -2, f2515 +3, f3581 -2, f3690 -2), against about 8 for random frames; the hidden scale jumps at f3094, f3134 and f3214 sit 7-11 f after the tracked beat. The capture's audio delay is unknown (UNCERTAIN by a constant). Original typefaces are not identifiable from the grayscale capture; the closest free families are given (Inter for UI and captions, a condensed grotesque for the S05 headline).

**3D:** soft grey spheres in the background with heavy depth-of-field blur, matte, no specular light; they rearrange during the opening (f0-f26, on twos) and then only drift (1-4 luma change per 10 f, magnitude UNCERTAIN). The shards grow while flying out (toward the camera), flat-shaded. No camera orbit.
**Graphic elements and UI:** label box with selection-style corner handles; brand mark icon and app icon inside the text; mascot balls with glyph eyes in 5 tones (light grey tail to near-black lead); thin arrow; pixel pair; double-triangle mark; halftone ring; luminance-inverting halo rings; shard burst. Geometry (box, balls, pixel pair, background) poses on twos (every 5 frames, phase f1 + 5k); arrow, fills and letters on every picture.
**Speed changes:** opening pop decelerates (key steps 260 -> 146 -> 66 px); the halftone ring decelerates the whole way out, 39 px/f (f0-f6) -> 18.5 (f6-f16) -> 9.2 (f16-f31) -> 7.1 (f31-f46) -> 3.9 px/f (f46-f56), and leaves the frame without settling; the widen overshoots then settles; inside it the content slides apart on every picture with a slow start and one big step (brand mark icon 9% W between f19 and f21, motion-blurred), then eases out by f26-f29; the line-1 rise (f34-f51) has the same shape over 17 f (1.5% H step f39-f41); slow accelerating push-in (f89-f131) breaks into a 5-frame punch (x1.23, f131-f136) and then slows to a steady drift (f136-f156); ring 1 accelerates 14 px/f (f129-f134) -> 97 px/f (f139-f141); the iris grows exponentially; then a 7-frame white hold (f162-f168).
**Transition out -> S02:** T01, mask wipe (iris to white + hold), 25 frames (f144-f168); shares: the white field (luma 248) that is S02's background; the disc is centred on the label centre (x 49.9%, y 50.2%) where the mascot chain landed; the next layout appears on the white at f169 from the top-left, scaled x1.59 and blurred; curve: curves/v7.trans-iris-white.csv. Preceded by the two inverting rings (f129-f151).
**Sound sync:** word 2's faint first letter f1 = beat f15 -14; brand mark draw-on f4 / closed f9 = beat f15 -11 / -6 (not synced); label widen starts f14 = strong onset f14 +0 (strength 8.6, the strongest of f0-f709), beat f15 -1; slide-apart starts f16 = beat f15 +1 (onset f14 +2); line-1 rise starts f34 = beat f43 -9; line 2 starts f49 = beat f43 +6; chain enters f81 = beat f69 +12 (between beats); ball lands / ring 1 f126 / f129 = beat f125 +1 / +4; iris starts f144 = beat f157 -13; frame white f162 = beat f157 +5.

**3D:** glossy 3D pointer: rounded triangular arrowhead with a dark-to-light vertical gradient, a thin bright rim and a soft bloom; 248 x 257 px (15.3% W x 28.0% H), centre 51.8% W / 51.7% H (still f1341). It appears tilted toward the camera and foreshortened (f1304-f1318) and turns to a frontal view by about f1330 (angles UNCERTAIN, no 3D solve). Depth of field: the UI behind is unreadably soft the whole time. Parallax in the spin-in push: the cursor scales x3.42 while the background scales x2.58 (the cursor sits on a nearer plane, or also grows). Capsule: glossy pill with a vertical gradient (lighter bottom) and a thin light outline, 627 x 124 px (38.7% W x 13.5% H) on the still f1425, 6 f before it reaches its rest width: half-level edge crossings give 627.9 x 125.8 px at f1425 and 637.7 x 125.8 px (39.3% W x 13.7% H) at rest from f1431 (scratch/fix_s11_capsule_left_1410-1450.csv, scratch/fix_s11_capsule_right_1410-1450.csv, scratch/fix_s11_capsule_top_1410-1446.csv, scratch/fix_s11_capsule_bottom_1410-1446.csv).
**Graphic elements and UI:** cursor, 3D pointer, status capsule, typed label, ellipsis.
**Speed changes:** decelerating spin-in (21 f) -> hold -> linear glow ramp (17 f) with an accelerating push (x1.10) -> one-picture swap with a flash -> snap back and a slow 40 f pull-back (x0.92) while the glow decays -> hold (f1349-f1364) -> capsule birth and camera reframe overlapping (45 f) while the typing speeds up -> no real hold: the reframe's tail (pointer 488 / 487 / 486 / 486 / 485 px at f1416 / 1419 / 1421 / 1423 / 1425) turns at f1426 into the foreground's slow drift (2 px per picture to f1431, while the capsule finishes widening behind the third dot: +6.0 / +3.0 / +0.8 px at f1426 / 1429 / 1431; then 4 / 4 / 5 / 6 / 6 / 8 / 8 / 10 / 11 / 12 px per picture to f1456), which accelerates into the whip (58 f to the last picture) in which the foreground and the background move in opposite directions (orbit-like parallax).
**Transition out -> S12:** T11, hard cut f1487 (c033) inside the second leftward whip, range f1426-f1514 (89 f: the foreground's slow drift from f1426, 2 px per picture, then the outgoing curve's 2% window from f1433). Outgoing: the foreground (pointer + capsule) slides left and up with growing blur while the defocused background drifts right (+63 px), so the move reads as a camera orbit, not a flat pan. Incoming: the file window arrives moving left at about 88 px per frame (177 px over the first 2 frames, 5.5% W per frame), 3.7x faster than the outgoing end, and decelerates over 27 f; its chrome is sharp (blur 1 px) while the thumbnail grid carries 91 px (5.6% W) of horizontal blur. Shares: leftward motion and the pointer motif (the glowing 3D pointer -> a small dark 3D pointer at the first thumbnail). Detail: detail/soft_1433-1514.
**Sound sync:** cut f1259 = beat f1254 +5; glow ramp start f1284 = beat f1284 (0); swap f1304 = beat f1314 -10; dot birth f1366 = beat f1375 -9; reframe start f1371 = beat f1375 -4; first character f1391 = beat f1402 -11; label complete f1416 = beat f1402 +14; ellipsis complete f1425 = beat f1431 -6; foreground drift starts f1426 = beat f1431 -5; whip acceleration f1433 = beat f1431 +2; cut f1487 = beat f1490 -3.

**Speed changes:** whip-in deceleration (24 f) -> hold with a fast stepped selection sweep (19 f) -> dim step (f1586) -> pointer moves (f1591-f1601) -> the drag starts slowly and accelerates (3 -> 37 px per frame in 27 f, 62 px per frame at f1634-f1636) while the chrome (camera) eases in to the left at 27 px per frame: two opposite accelerating exits into the cut.
**Transition out -> S14:** T13, hard cut f1644 (c038, `v7.trans-whip-push-left`); last picture f1641 (repeated at f1642-f1643) -> first picture f1644. Outgoing chrome moving left at 27 px (1.7% W) per frame; incoming editor window arrives moving left at about 33 px (2.0% W) per frame with 44 px of horizontal blur on its edges. Shares: leftward motion, the light banded desktop background (it moves with the window, i.e. a camera pan, measured in S14), a dark rounded window on the left-centre. Detail: detail/cut_c038_f01644 (f1626-f1674), detail/peak_f01635.
**Sound sync:** cut f1542 = beat f1547 -5; first highlight f1556 = beat f1547 +9; last highlight f1575 = beat f1577 -2; dim step f1586 = beat f1577 +9; drag start f1604 = beat f1608 -4; chrome starts moving ~f1606 (-2 vs beat f1608); cut f1644 (+2 vs beat f1642, which is also an onset).
**Curves:** `curves/v7.trans-whip-push-left.i5.csv` bezier(0.466, 0.023, 1.000, 0.506) D=31 (delay -3); spring {51.2, 117.56, mass 1} rmse 0.415 (do not use); bezier rmse 0.026 · `curves/v7.ui-drag-group-out.csv` bezier(0.661, 0.010, 1.000, 0.874) D=33 (delay -3); spring {70.4, 59.09} rmse 0.403; bezier rmse 0.025. The whip-in at f1542 is the f1487 family (test fit bezier(0, 0.253, 0, 0.851) D=24, not stored); the selection sweep is stepped.

**Transition out -> S17:** T16, hard cut f2091 (c051, `v7.trans-punch-in-whip-up`): the cut jumps x1.76 closer (the large timecode label measures 0.5675 of its close-up size in S16, template scale search at f2041-f2089) onto the timeline and ghost outlines; the outgoing view pans up at 0.55% H per frame, the incoming close-up arrives 239 px (26.0% H) below its rest moving up at about 22 px (2.4% H) per frame with a vertical smear of about 90 px (10-90% width of the timecode label's top edge at f2091) and decelerates over 29 f. Shares: the same UI area (timeline tracks under the ghost outline grid) and the upward motion direction; the speed jumps about 2.5x across the cut (0.55% H x 1.76 = 0.96% H per frame before, 2.4% after); the ghost grid's downward drag relative to the timeline continues through the cut (y curve below; S17 movement table). Detail: detail/v7c3_cut_2091, detail/cut_c051_f02091.
**Sound sync:** cut in f2007 (+2 vs beat f2005, also an onset); tilt start ~f2041 (+5 vs beat f2036); ghost outlines appear f2061 (-1 vs beat f2062); the drag starts f2066 (+4); the drag's fastest part f2071-f2079 brackets onset f2078; cursor: first picture f2012 (+7 vs beat f2005), at rest f2045 (+9 vs beat f2036), drag start f2066 (+4 vs beat f2062), fastest f2079-f2081 (+1 / +3 vs onset f2078); cut out f2091 (-2 vs beat f2093).

**3D:** none.
**Graphic elements and UI:** the picture card (rounded 16:9 frame, no stroke) is the only new element. The scan band is a light vertical strip (luma +35-48 over the picture mid-card, +75-91 where it is compressed against either card edge) with a fine vertical streak texture. It enters as a glow along the card's left edge (f3371, f3426; a thin bright line in sweep 3), crosses left to right and dies against the right edge. Behind it (left) the card shows an edited version of the footage frame, ahead of it (right) the unedited one; each sweep reveals a different edit.
**Speed changes:** the pull-back is slow-fast-slow with its fastest pictures at f3274-f3279. The push-in into the card creeps, takes one fast picture (x1.141) and settles in a long tail. The camera then holds completely still for 157 f (card edges constant to 0.1 px at f3376-f3533) while the three sweeps run 55 and 58 f apart; the whip-out starts from that dead stop.
**Transition out -> S30:** T29, hard cut f3581 (last old picture f3579; c070) inside a down-left whip, motion range f3535-f3624 (90 f), detail/soft_3535-3624. Outgoing: the full-frame card slides down-left, accelerating. Incoming: the cut lands on a video-editor product UI window that exactly fills the frame and at once zooms in about a fixed point above-right of the frame, (1799, -113) px = (110.9% W, -12.3% H), so its top-right corner travels down-left into the frame. Shares: the down-left direction of motion and a dark rounded frame that fills the picture (card edges about 3% inside the frame -> window edges at the frame edges); no element is shared.
**Sound sync:** pull-back start f3259: beat and onset f3263 -4; fastest picture f3276: onset f3278 -2. Card first picture f3279: onset f3278 +1 (beat f3263 +16). Push-in start f3304: beat f3298 +6; fastest picture f3336: beat f3329 +7. Sweeps f3371 / 3426 / 3484: beats f3360 +11 / f3417 +9 / f3474 +10 (f3474 is a strong onset; the nearest onset to f3484 is f3485, -1). Whip-out start f3535: beat f3526 +9, onset f3536 -1. Cut f3581: beat and onset f3583 -2.

**3D:** the pointer is a small 3D arrow (soft rim light, slight glow). It turns while rising to the button and tumbles with smear on exit; rotation angles not solved (UNCERTAIN).
**Graphic elements and UI:** the button's hover and press: its pill fills light, peaks with a soft glow that spills left along the top bar, then fades; the camera recoils at the same time (x0.857). The viewer is not a footage layer: it holds one still picture for the whole segment (a frozen footage frame; its content is not described). The camera never rests while the viewer is in frame except f3649-f3654, so every pair of pictures was compared with the camera move compensated. With the S30 zoom about (1799, -113) px and the stored scales, the viewer picture differs between captured pictures by 0.8-2.0 luma mean abs difference (17 pairs in f3584-f3611, e.g. f3584 -> f3596 2.0, f3586 -> f3600 1.6, f3586 -> f3611 1.9; raw 11-30 luma); ECC on the UI alone (viewer masked) finds the same scale ratios within 0.12% and gives 0.6-2.1 luma; moving the warped picture by 5 / 20 px raises it to 2.6-5.6 / 7.1-8.6 luma. Later pictures scaled down onto the single wide picture f3581 match at 3.1-4.1 luma with a 2 px x offset at that scale (inside the f3581 zoom UNCERTAIN; 5 px vertical offsets give 9.7-12.8). The strip that comes back at the left edge in the recoil is the same picture as the viewer before it left the frame (f3600 / f3609 -> f3649: 1.2 / 1.0 luma, registered on the panel labels) and stays the same while the push-out carries it out (0.3-1.3 luma between f3644 and f3666; a 5 px move gives 1.8-2.9); at the near-rest f3649 -> f3654 it changes 1.1 luma raw. The first draft's 11.3 luma (f3649 -> f3666 on x 0-60) measured the push-out (x1.08 by f3666; the viewer's edge moves from 77.5 to 17.5 px). Scripts scratch/v7c5_tools/viewer_frozen.py, viewer_frozen2.py; numbers scratch/v7c5_p5_viewer_frozen.csv, v7c5_p5_viewer_frozen_shift.csv, v7c5_p5_viewer_strip.csv; warp check scratch/v7c5_p5_viewer_warp.jpg.
**Speed changes:** the incoming zoom decays geometrically (24% -> 10% -> 4.6% per frame) and turns straight into the recoil without a hold; a 2-5 f hold; then an accelerating push to the cut. The pointer decelerates in and accelerates out.
**Transition out -> S31:** T30, zoom-through hard cut f3690 (last old picture f3686; f3687-f3689 repeat it; c074), motion range f3651-f3716 (66 f), detail/soft_3651-3716. Shares: a zoom-in about the frame centre on both sides with the speed rising across the cut (5% -> 7% per frame), and a light-gray backdrop with large soft diagonal shapes on both sides; the 3D pointer style returns with the dragged tile in S31.
**Sound sync:** pointer enters f3606: beat f3610 -4. Button lights f3629: beat f3639 -10; press peak f3642: beat f3639 +3, onset f3650 -8. Push-out start f3651: onset f3650 +1. Cut f3690: beat f3692 -2, strong onset f3691 -1.

## Technique index
| technique | category | segments | reference frames | curve | summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `v7.ui-label-pop-in` | ui | S01, S36 | f4522-f4547 | curves/v7.ui-label-pop-in.csv | the title label box is born as a 13 px seed dash at the frame centre after 5 white frames, grows to a 56 px bar and pops to 32.5% x 25.0% with an explosive ease-out by f4547, on twos; seen from its true start only in the loop replay (S36); the first play starts mid-pop at f0 (3.5% -> 32.6% W in 11 f) |
| `v7.ui-label-fill-darken` | ui | S01, S36 | f0-f44 | curves/v7.ui-label-fill-darken.csv | the label box fill builds in two stages: light grey 207 -> mid grey 90 luma while the box grows (f1-f21), a hold, then 90 -> 0 in 15 f as the text completes (spring {456.9, 45.68}) |
| `v7.bg-halftone-ring-burst` | bg | S01, S36 | f0-f56 | curves/v7.bg-halftone-ring-burst.csv | a soft ring band of halftone dots over a static square dot grid (pitch 0.89% W) is born with the label's seed dash at its centre and expands on a strong ease-out (inner edge 9.4 -> 52.8% W in 56 f, 39 -> 3.9 px/f), leaving through the frame edges without fading; blurred grey spheres behind rearrange on twos |
| `v7.ui-corner-handles` | ui | S01, S36 | f1-f66 | - | two small grey squares fly out diagonally from opposite corners of the label with its pop (motion-blurred), sit just outside the corners like selection handles, follow the resize while shrinking x0.74 with the content (37 -> 27-28 px), then shrink out over 10 f |

## Motion personality
A restless product-UI film shot like a screen camera: nearly every layout enters already moving (a whip-in, a zoom-out settle, a cut into a scroll or into a dropdown mid-opening) and settles on a strong ease-out, and nearly every exit accelerates with directional blur into the next cut (whips left or up, pull-backs, zoom-throughs), often around a hidden hard cut that jumps the same canvas closer or wider in one picture. Inside those moves the UI does small, quick, stepped things: letters type grey and turn white, caption words fade up with a small rise and de-blur, list items pop in one per picture, counters swap hard, cursors are thrown and land critically damped, and soft white blooms cover content swaps and edits. The opening title card (played again after the loop) is the playful exception, with geometry on twos, a scrambled word, a mascot chain that arcs and dives into a luminance-inverting ripple and a white iris, while footage stretches with voice-led captions sit between the UI sequences.
